), we expect the following natural propagator masses of

\displaystyle m_{(\mathbf{1}\,,\mathbf{6}\,,-\frac{1}{2})_{\mathbf{H}\,,\dot{1}}}\sim\mathcal{O}(v_{431})\,,

\displaystyle m_{(\mathbf{1}\,,\mathbf{6}\,,-\frac{1}{2})_{\mathbf{H}\,,\dot{2}}}\sim\mathcal{O}(v_{421})\,,

(190)

\displaystyle m_{(\mathbf{\overline{4}}\,,\mathbf{\overline{4}}\,,0)_{\mathbf{H}\,,\dot{1}}}\sim\mathcal{O}(v_{431})\,,

\displaystyle m_{(\mathbf{\overline{4}}\,,\mathbf{\overline{4}}\,,0)_{\mathbf{H}\,,\dot{2}}}\sim\mathcal{O}(v_{421})\,.

For convenience, we parametrize the following ratios of

\displaystyle\Delta_{\dot{\omega}}\equiv\frac{w_{\mathbf{\overline{4}}\,,\dot{1}}w_{\mathbf{\overline{4}}\,,\dot{\rm VII}}}{m_{(\mathbf{\overline{4}}\,,\mathbf{\overline{4}}\,,0)_{\mathbf{H}\,,\dot{\omega}}}^{2}}\,,\quad\Delta_{\dot{\omega}}^{\prime}\equiv\frac{w_{\mathbf{\overline{4}}\,,\dot{1}}w_{\mathbf{\overline{4}}\,,\dot{\rm VII}}}{m_{(\mathbf{1}\,,\mathbf{6}\,,-\frac{1}{2})_{\mathbf{H}\,,\dot{\omega}}}^{2}}\,.

(191)

C.6
The SM quark/lepton masses and the CKM mixing

For all up-type quarks with
Q_{e}=+\frac{2}{3}
, we write down the following tree-level masses from both the renormalizable Yukawa couplings and the gravity-induced terms in the basis of
\mathcal{U}\equiv(u\,,c\,,t)
:

\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{u}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\left(\begin{array}[]{ccc}0&c_{4}\dot{\zeta}_{3}^{\prime}/\sqrt{2}&c_{5}\zeta_{1}/\sqrt{2}\\
0&0&c_{5}\zeta_{3}/\sqrt{2}\\
c_{5}\zeta_{1}/\sqrt{2}&c_{5}\zeta_{3}/\sqrt{2}&Y_{\mathcal{T}}\\
\end{array}\right)v_{\rm EW}\approx\mathcal{M}_{u}^{(0)}+\mathcal{M}_{u}^{(1)}+\mathcal{M}_{u}^{(2)}\,,

\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{u}^{(0)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\left(\begin{array}[]{ccc}0&0&0\\
0&0&0\\
0&0&Y_{\mathcal{T}}\\
\end{array}\right)v_{\rm EW}\,,

\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{u}^{(1)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\left(\begin{array}[]{ccc}0&0&c_{5}\zeta_{1}/\sqrt{2}\\
0&0&0\\
c_{5}\zeta_{1}/\sqrt{2}&0&0\\
\end{array}\right)v_{\rm EW}\,,

\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{u}^{(2)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\left(\begin{array}[]{ccc}0&c_{4}\dot{\zeta}_{3}^{\prime}/\sqrt{2}&0\\
0&0&c_{5}\zeta_{3}/\sqrt{2}\\
0&c_{5}\zeta_{3}/\sqrt{2}&0\\
\end{array}\right)v_{\rm EW}\,,

where we have neglected the terms of
\sim\mathcal{O}(\zeta_{3}\,v_{\rm EW})
in the expansion.
One obvious feature is that the gauge eigenstates of the up quark and the charm quark do not obtain tree-level masses through the
d=5
operators with the SM Higgs doublet.
Instead, there are only off-diagonal mass mixing terms in Eqs. (
192
) and (
192
).
Accordingly, we find that

\displaystyle{\rm det}^{\prime}\left[\mathcal{M}_{u}^{(0)}\mathcal{M}_{u}^{(0)\,{\dagger}}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}Y_{\mathcal{T}}^{2}\,v_{\rm EW}^{2}\Rightarrow m_{t}^{2}\approx\frac{1}{2}Y_{\mathcal{T}}^{2}\,v_{\rm EW}^{2}\,.

(193)

Here and below, we use the
{\rm det}^{\prime}
to denote the matrix determinant that is equal to the products of all nonzero eigenvalues.
Next, we find that

\displaystyle{\rm det}^{\prime}\left[\Big(\mathcal{M}_{u}^{(0)}+\mathcal{M}_{u}^{(1)}\Big)\cdot\Big(\mathcal{M}_{u}^{(0)\,{\dagger}}+\mathcal{M}_{u}^{(1)\,{\dagger}}\Big)\right]

\displaystyle\approx

\displaystyle\frac{1}{4}c_{5}^{4}\zeta_{1}^{4}\,v_{\rm EW}^{4}\,.

(194)

Naturally, we expect the smaller eigenvalue above to be the charm quark mass squared of

\displaystyle m_{c}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\rm det}^{\prime}\left[\Big(\mathcal{M}_{u}^{(0)}+\mathcal{M}_{u}^{(1)}\Big)\cdot\Big(\mathcal{M}_{u}^{(0)\,{\dagger}}+\mathcal{M}_{u}^{(1)\,{\dagger}}\Big)\right]\Big/{\rm det}^{\prime}\left[\mathcal{M}_{u}^{(0)}\mathcal{M}_{u}^{(0)\,{\dagger}}\right]\approx\frac{c_{5}^{4}\zeta_{1}^{4}}{8Y_{\mathcal{T}}^{2}}\,v_{\rm EW}^{2}\,.

(195)

The up quark mass squared can be similarly obtained by

\displaystyle m_{u}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\rm det}\left[\Big(\mathcal{M}_{u}^{(0)}+\mathcal{M}_{u}^{(1)}+\mathcal{M}_{u}^{(2)}\Big)\cdot\Big(\mathcal{M}_{u}^{(0)\,{\dagger}}+\mathcal{M}_{u}^{(1)\,{\dagger}}+\mathcal{M}_{u}^{(2)\,{\dagger}}\Big)\right]

(196)

\displaystyle\Big/{\rm det}^{\prime}\left[\Big(\mathcal{M}_{u}^{(0)}+\mathcal{M}_{u}^{(1)}\Big)\cdot\Big(\mathcal{M}_{u}^{(0)\,{\dagger}}+\mathcal{M}_{u}^{(1)\,{\dagger}}\Big)\right]\approx\frac{c_{4}^{2}\zeta_{3}^{2}\dot{\zeta}_{3}^{2}}{4\zeta_{1}^{2}}\,v_{\rm EW}^{2}\,.

To summarize, all SM up-type quark masses are expressed as follows:

\displaystyle m_{u}\approx c_{4}\frac{\zeta_{3}\dot{\zeta}_{3}}{2\zeta_{1}}v_{\rm EW}\,,\quad m_{c}\approx c_{5}^{2}\frac{\zeta_{1}^{2}}{2\sqrt{2}Y_{\mathcal{T}}}v_{\rm EW}\,,\quad m_{t}\approx\frac{Y_{\mathcal{T}}}{\sqrt{2}}v_{\rm EW}\,.

(197)

For all down-type quarks with
Q_{e}=-\frac{1}{3}
, we find the following tree-level SM mass matrix:

\displaystyle\Big(\mathcal{M}_{d}\Big)_{3\times 3}\approx\frac{1}{4}\left(\begin{array}[]{ccc}(2c_{3}+Y_{\mathcal{D}}d_{\mathscr{B}})\dot{\zeta}_{3}&(2c_{3}+Y_{\mathcal{D}}d_{\mathscr{B}}\zeta_{23}^{-2})\dot{\zeta}_{3}&0\\
(2c_{3}+Y_{\mathcal{D}}d_{\mathscr{B}})\dot{\zeta}_{2}&(2c_{3}+Y_{\mathcal{D}}d_{\mathscr{B}}\zeta_{23}^{-2})\dot{\zeta}_{2}&0\\
0&0&Y_{\mathcal{B}}d_{\mathscr{A}}\zeta_{23}^{-1}\zeta_{1}\\
\end{array}\right)v_{\rm EW}\,.

This mass matrix has the similar structure as in Eq. (
B.6
) from the WSW pattern, while the terms in the second row receive the
\mathcal{G}_{431}
breaking VEVs of
w_{\mathbf{\overline{3}},\,\dot{1},\dot{\rm{VII}}}
.
It is straightforward to find the following SM down-type quark masses of

\displaystyle m_{b}

\displaystyle\approx

\displaystyle\frac{1}{4}Y_{\mathcal{B}}d_{\mathscr{A}}\zeta_{23}^{-1}\zeta_{1}\,v_{\rm EW}\,,

(202a)

\displaystyle m_{s}

\displaystyle\approx

\displaystyle\frac{1}{4}\left(2c_{3}+Y_{\mathcal{D}}d_{\mathscr{B}}\zeta_{23}^{-2}\right)\dot{\zeta}_{2}\,v_{\rm EW}\,,

(202b)

\displaystyle m_{d}

\displaystyle\approx

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}c_{3}\dot{\zeta}_{3}\,v_{\rm EW}\,,

(202c)

from Eq. (
C.6
).

For all charged leptons with
Q_{e}=-1
, their tree-level mass matrix is correlated with the down-type quark mass matrix as

\displaystyle\Big(\mathcal{M}_{\ell}\Big)_{3\times 3}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Big(\mathcal{M}_{d}^{T}\Big)_{3\times 3}

\displaystyle\approx

\displaystyle\frac{1}{4}\left(\begin{array}[]{ccc}(2c_{3}+Y_{\mathcal{D}}d_{\mathscr{B}})\dot{\zeta}_{3}&(2c_{3}+Y_{\mathcal{D}}d_{\mathscr{B}})\dot{\zeta}_{2}&0\\
(2c_{3}+Y_{\mathcal{D}}d_{\mathscr{B}}\zeta_{23}^{-2})\dot{\zeta}_{3}&(2c_{3}+Y_{\mathcal{D}}d_{\mathscr{B}}\zeta_{23}^{-2})\dot{\zeta}_{2}&0\\
0&0&Y_{\mathcal{B}}d_{\mathscr{A}}\zeta_{23}^{-1}\zeta_{1}\\
\end{array}\right)v_{\rm EW}\,.

Thus, it is straightforward to find the tree-level mass relations of

\displaystyle m_{\tau}=m_{b}\,,\quad m_{\mu}=m_{s}\,,\quad m_{e}=m_{d}\,.

(207)
